POD_make_artifact_unavailable_effect = {
	set_variable = unavailable_artifact
}

POD_create_book_artifact_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_ep1_dlc_trigger = yes }
		create_artifact_$artifact$_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ TYPE = book }
	}
	else = {
		create_artifact_$artifact$_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ TYPE = miscellaneous_when_not_court }
	}
}

# POD Artifacts Creation #

create_artifact_sword_of_nul_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_illustrious = yes
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = artifact_sword_of_nul_name
		description = artifact_sword_of_nul_description
		type = sword
		template = general_unique_template
		visuals = wall_POD_sword_of_nul 
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		modifier = sword_of_nul_modifier
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = sword_of_nul
		set_variable = possessed_artifact
		save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sword_of_nul
			value = yes
		}
		add_artifact_history = {
			type = conquest
			date = 20.1.1
			recipient = character:790422
		}
	}	

}

create_artifact_tablet_of_destiny_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_illustrious = yes
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = artifact_tablet_of_destiny_name
		description = artifact_tablet_of_destiny_description
		type = $TYPE$	
		template = tablet_of_destiny_template
		visuals = book_POD_tablet_of_destiny
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality			
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		modifier =  artifact_monthly_lifestyle_xp_20_modifier 
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no		
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = tablet_of_destiny
		save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = tablet_of_destiny
			value = yes
		}
	}	
}

		}

#chaining the beast p68
create_artifact_phrygian_codex_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes

	if = {
		limit = { has_ep1_dlc_trigger = yes }
		create_artifact = {
			decaying = no	
			name = artifact_phrygian_codex_name
			description = artifact_phrygian_codex_description
			type = book
			template = phrygian_codex_template	
			modifier = phrygian_codex_modifier
			visuals = book		
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
			history = { type = created_before_history }
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_artifact = {
			decaying = no	
			name = artifact_phrygian_codex_name
			description = artifact_phrygian_codex_description
			type = miscellaneous_when_not_court
			template = phrygian_codex_template	
			modifier = phrygian_codex_modifier
			visuals = book		
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
			history = { type = created_before_history }
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = phrygian_codex
		set_variable = usable_artifact
	}
}

		name = artifact_alashrad_eye_name
		description = artifact_alashrad_eye_description
		#category = inventory
		type = miscellaneous
		#template = vampire_unique_template
		modifier = artifact_learning_per_prestige_level_modifier
		visuals = pedestal_POD_alashrad_eye
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = alashrad_eye
		#save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = alashrad_eye
			value = yes
		}
	}

#TC 1 p73
create_artifact_librum_terrum_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	set_artifact_rarity_masterwork = yes

	if = {
		limit = { has_ep1_dlc_trigger = yes }
		create_artifact = {
			decaying = no	
			name = artifact_librum_terrum_name
			description = artifact_librum_terrum_description
			type = book
			template = general_unique_template
			visuals = book		
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
			history = { type = created_before_history }
			modifier = artifact_intrigue_2_modifier	
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_artifact = {
			decaying = no	
			name = artifact_librum_terrum_name
			description = artifact_librum_terrum_description
			type = miscellaneous_when_not_court
			template = general_unique_template
			visuals = book		
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
			history = { type = created_before_history }
			modifier = artifact_intrigue_2_modifier	
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
	
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = librum_terrum
	}		
}

#p222 veil of night and mummy books 
create_artifact_writings_cabirus_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_common = yes

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_writings_cabirus_name
		description = artifact_writings_cabirus_description
		#category = court
		type = $TYPE$
		template = general_unique_template	
		visuals = book		
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		modifier = artifact_monthly_melancholy_lifestyle_xp_5_modifier			
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = writings_cabirus
		#save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = writings_cabirus
			value = yes
		}
	}		
}

#V5 forgotten religions
create_artifact_shattered_spear_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_shattered_spear_name
		description = artifact_shattered_spear_description
		#category = inventory
		type = spear
		visuals = spear	
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}	
		modifier = shattered_spear_modifier 				
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = shattered_spear
		#save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = shattered_spear
			value = yes
		}
	}	
}
#Demons hunters x
create_artifact_bow_of_yi_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_illustrious = yes
	set_variable = {
		name = artifact_bow_type
		value = flag:artifact_bow_type_bow
	}
	set_bow_artifact_type_effect = {
		BASE_SCOPE = scope:owner
		NO_TYPE = no
	}
	scope:owner = {
		var:artifact_bow_type = { save_scope_as = bow_type }
		remove_variable = artifact_bow_type
	}

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_bow_of_yi_name
		description = artifact_bow_of_yi_description
		type = bow
		visuals = bow
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		template = general_unique_template
		modifier = artifact_prowess_per_prestige_3_modifier
		modifier = artifact_monthly_qiao_lifestyle_xp_5_modifier			
		modifier = artifact_archers_damage_5_modifier			
		modifier = artifact_archer_cavalry_damage_5_modifier			
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = bow_of_yi
	}
}

create_artifact_djangalbi_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = artifact_djangalbi_name
		description = artifact_djangalbi_description
		type = $TYPE$		
		template = general_unique_template
		visuals = indian_book
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality		
		modifier = djangalbi_modifier
		history = {
			type = created
			date = 1166.12.5
			actor = character:560126
			recipient = character:560126
		}
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no	
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		#set_variable = djangalbi
		#save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = djangalbi
			value = yes
		}

	}	
}
#mummy book
create_artifact_sets_emasculation_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_sets_emasculation_name
		description = artifact_sets_emasculation_description
		#category = inventory
		type = sword
		template = general_unique_template
		visuals = sword_POD_sheriffsword_b
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		modifier = sets_emasculation_modifier
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = sets_emasculation
		save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sets_emasculation
			value = yes
		}
		add_to_global_variable_list = {
			name = egyptian_artifacts
			target = this
		}
	}	
}
#devil's due
create_artifact_vassagos_reliquary_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = artifact_vassagos_reliquary_name
		description = artifact_vassagos_reliquary_description
		type = $TYPE$
		# template = vassagos_reliquary_template	
		visuals = diamond
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality		
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}		
		modifier = artifact_monthly_prestige_gain_per_dread_add_4_modifier
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no		
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = possessed_artifact 
		#add_soulforged_artifact_modifier_effect = { KILLED = root }
		add_artifact_modifier = artifact_dread_gain_mult_2_modifier
		set_max_durability = 666	
		set_variable = vassagos_reliquary		
	}
}


POD_create_artifact_human_skull_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	
	$CREATOR$ = { save_scope_as = creator }
	
	$DESKULLED$ = { save_scope_as = killed }

	get_artifact_quality_effect = yes
	get_artifact_wealth_effect = yes

	scope:owner = {
		create_artifact = {
			name = artifact_pedestal_human_skull_name
			creator = scope:creator
			description = artifact_pedestal_human_skull_description
			visuals = human_skull
			type = $TYPE$
			modifier = artifact_dread_gain_mult_1_modifier
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
			#decaying = no
		}
	}
	hidden_effect_new_object = {
		scope:newly_created_artifact = {
			add_scaled_artifact_modifier_grandeur_small_effect = yes
			add_scaled_artifact_modifier_minor_prestige_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					rarity = illustrious
				}
				add_artifact_modifier = artifact_dread_baseline_add_2_modifier
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					rarity = famed
				}
				add_artifact_modifier = artifact_dread_baseline_add_1_modifier
			}
		}
	}
}

create_artifact_blazing_silver_torc_of_myfanwy_du_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = artifact_blazing_silver_torc_of_myfanwy_du_name
		description = artifact_blazing_silver_torc_of_myfanwy_du_description
		type = necklace	
		visuals = torc
		template = crafted_trasure_artwork_unique_template
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		modifier = artifact_attraction_opinion_4_modifier
		modifier = artifact_seduce_scheme_power_add_4_modifier
		modifier = artifact_courting_scheme_power_add_4_modifier
		modifier = artifact_sway_scheme_power_add_4_modifier
		modifier = artifact_spouse_opinion_add_4_modifier
		decaying = no
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = enchantment 
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
	}
}

create_artifact_goblin_bow_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_masterwork = yes
	
	set_variable = {
		name = artifact_bow_type
		value = flag:artifact_bow_type_crossbow
	}
	set_bow_artifact_type_effect = {
		BASE_SCOPE = scope:owner
		NO_TYPE = no
	}
	scope:owner = {
		var:artifact_bow_type = { save_scope_as = bow_type }
		remove_variable = artifact_bow_type
	}
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = artifact_goblin_bow_name
		description = artifact_goblin_bow_description
		type = crossbow
		visuals = goblin_bow
		template = crafted_trasure_weapon_template
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		modifier = artifact_prowess_5_modifier
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact

		name = artifact_passion_braceler_name
		description = artifact_passion_braceler_description
		template = imbued_trasure_artwork_template
		type = miscellaneous
		visuals = bracelet
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		modifier = artifact_stress_gain_4_modifier
		modifier = artifact_monthly_lifestyle_xp_1_modifier
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = enchantment 
		POD_energy_vessel_add_full_energy_bar_as_max = yes
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
	}
}

create_artifact_eilaf_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes
	set_variable = {
		name = artifact_bow_type
		value = flag:artifact_bow_type_bow
	}
	set_bow_artifact_type_effect = {
		BASE_SCOPE = scope:owner
		NO_TYPE = no
	}
	scope:owner = {
		var:artifact_bow_type = { save_scope_as = bow_type }
		remove_variable = artifact_bow_type
	}
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		name = artifact_eilaf_name
		description = artifact_eilaf_description
		type = bow
		visuals = bow
		template = eilaf_template
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		modifier = artifact_prowess_9_modifier
		modifier = artifact_pursue_efficiency_6_modifier
		modifier = artifact_archers_damage_5_modifier
		modifier = artifact_archer_cavalry_damage_5_modifier
		modifier = artifact_army_pursuit_mult_5_modifier
		decaying = no
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = enchantment 
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = eilaf	
	}
}

		modifier = artifact_character_travel_speed_1_modifier
		template = animal_template
		max_durability = 10
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}

		set_variable = { name = pet_artifact value = flag:tiger }
	}
}

create_artifact_to_create_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	character:POD_wraith_01 = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_$RARITY$ = yes
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {	
		name = artifact_to_create_name
		description = artifact_to_create_name
		visuals = necklace
		type = miscellaneous
		modifier = artifact_placeholder_modifier
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no
		template = general_unique_template
		history = { type = created_before_history } 
	}
	
	hidden_effect_new_object = {	
		scope:newly_created_artifact = {
			remove_artifact_modifier = artifact_placeholder_modifier
			set_artifact_pieces_modifier_effect = yes
			set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = $RARITY$_artifact_to_create
			value = scope:newly_created_artifact
		}
	}
}

		visuals = armor
		modifier = artifact_prowess_5_modifier	
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}	
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = elemental_armor
		#save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = elemental_armor
			value = yes
		}
	}		
}

#TC4 p22
create_artifact_incunabulum_kupalam_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	set_artifact_rarity_illustrious = yes

	if = {
		limit = { has_ep1_dlc_trigger = yes }
		create_artifact = {
			name = artifact_incunabulum_kupalam_name
			description = artifact_incunabulum_kupalam_description
			type = book 		
			template = koldun_book_template
			visuals = pocket_book
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
			modifier = incunabulum_kupalam_modifier
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
			history = { type = created_before_history }		
			decaying = no	
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_artifact = {
			name = artifact_incunabulum_kupalam_name
			description = artifact_incunabulum_kupalam_description
			type = miscellaneous_when_not_court		
			template = koldun_book_template
			visuals = pocket_book
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality		
			modifier = incunabulum_kupalam_modifier
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
			history = { type = created_before_history }
			decaying = no	
		}
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = incunabulum_kupalam
	}	
}

#al ashrad bio in COTN
create_artifact_bow_sun_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_masterwork = yes
	set_variable = {
		name = artifact_bow_type
		value = flag:artifact_bow_type_bow
	}
	set_bow_artifact_type_effect = {
		BASE_SCOPE = scope:owner
		NO_TYPE = no
	}
	scope:owner = {
		var:artifact_bow_type = { save_scope_as = bow_type }
		remove_variable = artifact_bow_type
	}

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_bow_sun_name
		description = artifact_bow_sun_description
		type = bow
		visuals = bow
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		template = general_unique_template
		modifier = crones_tongue_modifier			
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
			actor = character:790166
		}			
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		#set_variable = bow_sun
	}
}

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_intrigue_shard_name
		description = artifact_intrigue_shard_description
		#category = court
		type = $TYPE$
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}			
		template = shard_template	
		visuals = book_POD_black		
		modifier = sagron_codex_shard_modifier
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		add_artifact_modifier = sargon_codex_shard_intrigue_modifier
		#set_variable = book_tobit
		#ave_scope_as = epic
		#save_scope_value_as = {
		#	name = book_tobit
		#	value = yes
		#}
	}
}

create_artifact_day_armor_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_masterwork = yes

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_day_armor_name
		description = artifact_day_armor_description
		#category = court
		type = armor_plate
		template = spiritus_advanced_template
		#visuals = wall_POD_day_armor  #Doesn't show up in the royal court
		visuals = armor
		modifier = day_armor_modifier

		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = day_armor
		#save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = day_armor
			value = yes
		}
	}	
}

		description = artifact_ki_chuan_description

		type = $TYPE$
		visuals = indian_book

		modifier = ki_chuan_modifier

		history = { type = created_before_history }
		
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = { set_variable = historical_unique_artifact }	
}

create_artifact_knives_of_subline_hunger_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes
	
	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		template = deviltrigers_dharma_template

		name = artifact_knives_of_subline_hunger_name
		description = artifact_knives_of_subline_hunger_description

		type = sword
		visuals = dagger

		modifier = knives_of_subline_hunger_modifier

		decaying = no	
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality

		history = { type = created_before_history }
		
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = { set_variable = historical_unique_artifact }
}

create_artifact_taming_sari_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_illustrious = yes

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		decaying = no	
		name = artifact_taming_sari_name
		description = artifact_taming_sari_description
		#category = inventory
		type = dagger
		template = general_unique_template	
		visuals = dagger 
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		modifier = ainkurn_modifier 				
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact
		set_variable = taming_sari
		#save_scope_as = epic
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = taming_sari
			value = yes
		}
	}
}

		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		modifier = dragon_night_modifier

		decaying = no
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {

		save_scope_as = epic

			name = dragon_night
			value = yes
		}
	}	

}

		visuals = indian_book

		modifier = artifact_wanderer_lifestyle_modifier

		decaying = no	
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality

		history = { type = created_before_history }
		
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = { set_variable = historical_unique_artifact }	
}

#Prias char entry
create_artifact_eternal_love_effect = {
	# Get the character the artifact is being made for.
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	# Not really used, but if we don't set the scopes we get errors in the feature selection
	set_artifact_rarity_masterwork = yes

	# Create the artifact
	create_artifact = {
		template = general_unique_template

		name = eternal_love_artifact_name
		description = eternal_love_artifact_description
		decaying = no
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		history = {
			type = created_before_history
		}
		type = sword
		visuals = sword
		modifier = bright_arm_modifier		
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = { 
		set_variable = historical_unique_artifact 
		set_variable = eternal_love
	}	
}
